Existing source scope does not implicitly authorize this new file.

**Firmware telemetry storage (source-verified):** `src/drivers/usart1.c` allocates static volatile `rx_irq_count`, `rx_byte_count`, `rx_drop_count`, `rx_error_count`, `rx_high_water` and 128-byte RX ring in SRAM. `usart1_init()` explicitly zeroes counters; `usart1_irq_service()` increments **one** error count for an ISR entry observing any of USART `PE|FE|NE|ORE` bits; this does **not** record which bit(s), byte, timestamp, nor 197 individual messages, and does not imply 197 lost bytes. `usart1_diagnostics_get()` / `rxstat` read live counters without resetting them. Stack high-water and guard state (Cortex-M MSP plus scheduler task stacks), USB/CDC/Management packet counters, runtime health/ticks, application and last fault record are primarily RAM data; `fault_record` (`kernel.c`) captures registers in SRAM then halts on fault, not a persistent crash log. Normal application boot initializes SRAM state, so no claim that such counters survive reset/power loss. `HEALTH RESET_FLAGS` captures hardware reset-cause flags at boot into RAM, and is not a complete historical log. The `197` count uses **one 4-byte counter**, not 197 accumulating record allocations or Flash writes.

**Durable Flash is a different owner:** `docs/FLASH_OWNERSHIP_LAYOUT_DECISION.md` keeps `0x08000000..0x08001FFF` bootloader, `0x08002000..0x0800EFFF` application, `0x0800F000..0x0800F7FF` dual firmware metadata pages and `0x0800F800..0x0800FFFF` A/B persistent asset/config records. General UART/error/test log storage is NOT implemented in these partitions; writing debug counters there would require a separate, explicitly authorized policy with erase wear, record layout, and ownership review. Firmware **contains** built-in diagnostic commands/tests as program code; their transient runtime results are not archived by the MCU. Operator `deus-run` evidence ZIP (`run.log`, `outcome.txt`, raw stdout/stderr, SHA manifest) is saved on the **Windows host Downloads**; a Linux `/tmp` scratch area is copied back and deleted after the bounded run. Published repository docs are descriptions, not a real-time MCU telemetry database.
